chancellor = {
	default = {
		default = { head = "council_chancellor" torso = "council_chancellor" }

		idle = {
			animation = { head = "dead" torso = "dead" }
			weight = {
				base = 0

				#	add = 32767
				#	character:33922 ?= this
				#}
			}
		}
		
		portrait_modifier = {
			#trigger = { NOT = { character:33922 ?= this } }
			animations = council_chancellor_decal
			animations_props = chancellor_prop
		}

		#Warcraft
		still = {
			animation = { head = "still" torso = "still" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200000
					has_no_animations_trigger = yes
				}

# Choking to death
poison = {
	default = {
		default = { head = "emotion_poison" torso = "emotion_poison" }

		#Warcraft
		still = {
			animation = { head = "still" torso = "still" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200000
					has_no_animations_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		portrait_modifier = {
			animations = emotion_poison_decal
		}

		emotion_poison = {
			animation = { head = "emotion_poison" torso = "emotion_poison" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					OR = {
						AND = { # Regular
							scope:current_weight <= 40
							#Warcraft
							NOT = { is_dwarf_height = yes }
							NOT = { portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant } }
						}
						AND = { # Pregnant
							scope:current_weight <= 40
							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
							#Warcraft
							NOT = { is_dwarf_height = yes }
						}
					}
				}
			}
		}

		emotion_poison_fat = { # Fat, Dwarf, FatDwarf, FatDwarfPregnant
			animation = { head = "emotion_poison" torso = "emotion_poisonFatDwarf" }
		}
	}

}

### Child

# Hands over pregnant belly
pregnant = {
	default = {
		default = { head = "idle" torso = "idle" }

		#Warcraft
		still = {
			animation = { head = "still" torso = "still" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200000
					has_no_animations_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
	}

	female = {
		default = { head = "misc_pregnant_entry" torso = "misc_pregnant_entry" }

		#Warcraft
		still = {
			animation = { head = "still" torso = "still" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200000
					has_no_animations_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}

		pregnant_basic = {
			animation = { head = "misc_pregnant_entry" torso = "misc_pregnant_entry" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					AND = {
						scope:current_weight < 60
						portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
						#Warcraft
						NOT = { is_dwarf_height = yes }
					}
				}
			}
			portrait_modifier = {
				animations = emotion_happy_decal
			}
		}

		pregnant_dwarf = {
			animation = { head = "misc_pregnant_entry" torso = "misc_pregnantFat_entry" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					OR = {
						AND = {
							scope:current_weight < 60
							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
							#Warcraft
							is_dwarf_height = yes
						}
						AND = {
							scope:current_weight >= 60
							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
						}
					}
				}
			}
			portrait_modifier = {
				animations = emotion_happy_decal
			}
		}

		pregnant_fat_dwarf = {
			animation = { head = "misc_pregnant_entry" torso = "misc_pregnantFatDwarf_entry" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					AND = {
						scope:current_weight >= 60
						portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
						#Warcraft
						is_dwarf_height = yes
					}
				}
			}
			portrait_modifier = {
				animations = emotion_happy_decal
			}
		}
	}
	girl = female
}

				}
			}
		}

			animation = { head = "divine_ecstasy" torso = "divine_ecstasy" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					spiritual_fulfillment >= 60
				}
			}
		}
		zealous_superior = {
			animation = { head = "zealous_superior" torso = "zealous_superior" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					spiritual_fulfillment >= 20
					spiritual_fulfillment < 60
				}
			}
		}
		happiness = {
			animation = { head = "emotion_happy_entry" torso = "emotion_happy_entry" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					spiritual_fulfillment > -20
					spiritual_fulfillment < 20
				}
			}
		}
		fear = {
			animation = { head = "emotion_fear_entry" torso = "emotion_fear_entry" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					spiritual_fulfillment <= -20
					spiritual_fulfillment > -60
				}
			}
		}
		mental_agony = {
			animation = { head = "mental_agony" torso = "mental_agony" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 100
					spiritual_fulfillment <= -60
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

					}
				}

					add = 100
					OR = {
						should_use_fat_animation = yes
						AND = { # Pregnant
							scope:current_weight < 60
							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
							#Warcraft
							NOT = { is_dwarf_height = yes }                           
						}
					}
				}
			}
			portrait_modifier_pack = two_handed_spear

					add = 100
					OR = {
						should_use_fat_dwarf_animation = yes
						AND = { # Pregnant Dwarf

							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
							#Warcraft
							is_dwarf_height = yes
						}
						AND = { # Pregnant Fat Dwarf
							scope:current_weight >= 60
							portrait_has_trait_trigger = { TRAIT = pregnant }
							#Warcraft
							is_dwarf_height = yes
						}
					}
				}
			}
			portrait_modifier_pack = two_handed_spear

	default = {
		default = { head = "horse_archer_idle" torso = "horse_archer_idle" }

		#Warcraft
		still = {
			animation = { head = "still" torso = "still" }
			weight = {
				base = 0
				modifier = {
					add = 200000
					has_no_animations_trigger = yes
				}
			}
		}
		
		horse_archer_idle = {
			animation = { head = "horse_archer_idle" torso = "horse_archer_idle" }
			portrait_modifier_pack = horse_archer_short_bow_without_arrow
		}
	}
}
